# Requirements v3 — sprint 2 scope

Date: 2026-09-22, §1–§2 and §5–§6 rendered 2026-09-24. Author: PM session (Fable), Stage A of the sprint 2 planning prompt
(`project-state/ORCHESTRATOR.md` §6, "Sprint 2 — planning"). Product manager: Stack.
Status: **DRAFT for Stack's read — §1 (81 carried requirements, R-29..R-109), §2 (65 PM-added + 48 research-added steps), §3 (his eight items, fields to confirm), §4 (still declined), §5 (54 items closed at Stage A), §6 (49 questions carried to Stage B's batch).** Phase briefs may
cite R-numbers from this file once Stack has confirmed it (Stage C).

This file continues `../sprint-1-hub/60_REQUIREMENTS_v2.md` (R-01..R-28). It does not restate
what sprint 1 delivered — STATUS's "Sprint 1 — closed" section is that record. It states what
sprint 1 **left undone**, as requirements (outcomes), never as the old prompts or task lists;
what Stack adds new; and what stays declined so nothing sneaks back. R-numbers continue from
**R-29**. Migrations for sprint 2 start at **091**.

How to read an entry in §1:

* **Source** — the sprint 1 document and row the requirement comes from.
* **State today** — one of *not built* · *partly built* (with what exists) · *built but
  unproven* (code merged, acceptance never walked) · *done* (listed only so it can be struck
  from STATUS). Every state was checked against the repo at `main` (`5246438`), prod, or the
  harness repo on 2026-09-22, not read off a document.
* **Still missing** — what has to exist before the requirement is met.
* **Must respect** — the sprint 1 decision (DECISIONS.md row, quoted) or frozen brief answer the
  work cannot reverse without a new DECISIONS row from Stack.

## 1. Carried requirements

Grouped by area, numbered in reading order from R-29. Every entry was extracted from the sprint 1 record by nine readers, merged, then verified item by item against the repo, prod and the harness (2026-09-22/24) and reviewed against the decision log; the HTML comment under each entry names the verified items it covers (the Stage A record in the PM's session).

The web helpers already accept the kind (queries.sync.ts:71-78, createAgentRequest 1193-1213). The 2 transform requests on prod (#10, #11) both date from 2026-09-15. Answer #544 (ECN.304/quiz-3 due date), resolved 2026-09-23 20:15Z after the last fold, was still unapplied on 2026-09-24, while INBOX_APPLY_HELP (queries.sync.ts:698-702) promises 'within a couple of minutes'.

#### R-54 · A planner series row never outlives its last occurrence
planner · db · size S · **not built**
* **Source:** planner-orphan-series-plain-delete: docs/planning/sprint-1-hub/walks/80o_PHASE12B_TAIL_WALK.md:36-38 finding W-3 ('open, known issue'); docs/planning/sprint-2/90_SPRINT2_INTAKE.md:17 S2-carry-5 ('S (trigger)'); project-state/STATUS.md:329 (row 15) and :417 (known issues); filed under Stack's S2-bugs-1 (91_REQUIREMENTS_v3.md:138).
* **State today:** 088 deletes an emptied series only inside the RPCs (planner_series_update / planner_series_delete 'following' at 088:161-164 and 227-231; 'all' at 232-245). The plain path, useDeletePlannerEvent (web/src/lib/queries.plannerEvents.ts:455-481), deletes the planner_events row and leaves the series. Two UI routes reach it: a detached row's plain delete (usePlannerEventEditor.ts:207-212) and 'This event' on the last attached member (:219-223). Prod 2026-09-24: five triggers on planner_events, none cleans up a series on DELETE; the FK series_id ON DELETE SET NULL works series-to-events only; planner_event_series 0 rows, 0 orphans; planner_events 1 row. The walk's one orphan was removed by SQL. db/tests/phase12b_082_083_planner_series.sql covers the RPC paths only (TR-4 at 594-633).
* **Still missing:** A migration adding an AFTER DELETE … REFERENCING OLD TABLE statement trigger on planner_events that deletes each series named in old_rows.series_id (nulls ignored) with zero rows left, never a cascade; search_path pinned to public, pg_temp, not SECURITY DEFINER, revoked from public/anon/authenticated as 082 does. A rolled-back SQL test: detached last row deleted gives 0 series; 'This event' on the last attached row gives 0 series; a series with rows left is untouched; TR-4 re-run under the trigger; a stranger uid deletes nothing. After apply, prod returns 0 for select count(*) from planner_event_series s where not exists (select 1 from planner_events e where e.series_id = s.id).
* **Must respect:** [2026-09-21] "**Recurring planner events are expanded in the web layer into ordinary `planner_events` rows** (082 `planner_event_series` holds only the rule; 083/088 RPCs write the rows the client sends). `calendar-push` and `v_calendar_push_items` are untouched; edits keep row ids so Google sees patches" · [2026-09-21] "**A series rule (frequency, end date) is not editable after creation; Repeats is offered on create only.** To change it: delete "this and following", create a new series. Weekly = same weekday; monthly = same day-of-month, a month without that day is skipped; 52 occurrences is a refusal, never a trim" · [2026-09-21] "**"All events" means every occurrence from now on, plus the one Stack opened.** The client cuts the RPC scope 60 s ahead (`SERIES_SCOPE_SAFETY_MS`) and writes the opened occurrence itself when it falls outside; a series edit never changes any row's `done`; a save with no changed column writes nothing and does not detach" · [2026-09-16] "The planner-event live proof ran on Stack's real `bb2dash` calendar with SQL-inserted `bb2dash test · …` rows, all deleted and pushed away in the same sitting (Google 64 = mirror 64 at the end); web workers never write `planner_events` on prod" · [2026-09-10] "Parallel phases get **non-overlapping migration ranges** (Phase 8 = 026–029, Phase 9 = 030–039) allocated in the briefs" · [2026-09-22] "**Sprint 2 planning follows the Phase 12b method** (list → ids → triage → researchers → one question batch → briefs); migration numbering continues from 091" · [2026-09-10] "RLS hardened to owner-scoped (migration 020): 21 `using(true)` authenticated policies → `auth.uid() = public.app_owner()`; storage `bb_files_auth_all` ANDed with the owner check; anon INSERT paths + service_role bypass preserved" · [2026-09-17 (Stack's answer 15, 80c_PHASE12B_page_pass.md:141-142)] "Recurring: daily / weekly / monthly, mandatory end date, ≤ 52 occurrences, deleting a series leaves past occurrences — **and single-occurrence edit/delete ships with it, not later.**"
* **Seams:** planner_events (082 series_id / series_detached, detached-implies-series check), planner_event_series (FK ON DELETE SET NULL, owner-only RLS, 082 §5), 083/088 RPCs (the statement trigger fires at the end of their inner DELETE, before their own series delete, which then matches 0 rows, so re-run TR-4 rather than assume), queries.plannerSeries.ts:411-415 deleteOpenedRow (series_id already nulled; the trigger must ignore nulls), planner_events_mark_calendar_dirty. The migration number must sit outside Phase 14's reserved 091-099 (ORCHESTRATOR:63, STATUS:445). Its browser acceptance (both last-row deletes, then the SELECT) is carried in B2-4's walk. Answers Stack's S2-bugs-1.
* **Notes:** Scope is wider than W-3's 'last detached occurrence': 'This event' on the last attached member also orphans a series; W-3, STATUS:329 and the intake title name only the route the walk hit. A series emptied by an UPDATE of series_id outside 088 is not covered by a DELETE trigger; the client never sends series_id on a plain update (queries.plannerEvents.ts:362), so the invariant holds in practice. The candidate invariant query as first written ('where not exists (...) = 0') is malformed; use the count form. A client-side follow-up delete is the weaker alternative (not atomic with the row delete).

Weekly = same weekday; monthly = same day-of-month, a month without that day is skipped; 52 occurrences is a refusal, never a trim" · [2026-09-21] "**"All events" means every occurrence from now on, plus the one Stack opened.** The client cuts the RPC scope 60 s ahead (`SERIES_SCOPE_SAFETY_MS`) and writes the opened occurrence itself when it falls outside; a series edit never changes any row's `done`; a save with no changed column writes nothing and does not detach" · [2026-09-16] "The planner-event live proof ran on Stack's real `bb2dash` calendar with SQL-inserted `bb2dash test · …` rows, all deleted and pushed away in the same sitting (Google 64 = mirror 64 at the end); web workers never write `planner_events` on prod" · [2026-09-16] "A planner event stores **instants plus its own IANA zone**; the web converts a wall clock to an instant once, with Temporal's `compatible` rule (a repeated fall-back time takes the earlier instant, a skipped spring-forward time moves forward), and SQL never re-derives an instant from a wall clock. Editing an event keeps its stored instants unless its date, time, zone or all-day setting changed (R2-4)" · [2026-09-16] "`planner_events.time_zone` must be `UTC` or an `Area/Location` name present in `pg_timezone_names`, checked by a trigger; the web sends `Intl`'s resolved name. Every other screen keeps the `?item=` popout. One dialog layer at a time on the week grid" · [2026-09-16] "Planner events are pushed to the `bb2dash` calendar as **ordinary events**; the kind lives in the title (`[IST 323 · ]Focus time · <title>`) and a fixed per-kind `colorId`, and a done task's title gains `✓ `" · [2026-09-14] "**Definition of done for every remaining phase = SOP gates + Stack's acceptance script** walked on the Vercel preview; sign-off once per phase, at the PR" · [2026-09-14] "**Every task carries an executable check** (test, SQL assertion, curl, screenshot diff) the worker runs itself, plus one demo line for the acceptance script; a task without a check is not a task"

* **Seams:** stage_gaps (034/041/054), attention_answered (041), archive_attention_item and v_inbox_queue (090), raise_attention, transform_tick, the owner's bb_files update handed out by ingest/pull_files.mjs (where storage_path actually clears), the /inbox-apply bucket table (dismissed rows are record-only, SKILL.md:83). #### R-65 · A sync folds only when complete and always shows its true state
db · ingest · web · containers · size M · **partly built**
* **Source:** 60_REQUIREMENTS_v2.md R-07 (:117-121) and R-08 (:122-124); briefs/62_PHASE9_sync_loop.md §Migration 035 (:82-89); verification/64_W15_VERIFICATION.md §10 (:445-462); 66_W17_VERIFICATION.md §12.2 R2-1 (:543-557); briefs/67_PHASE10A_grades.md R2-1 (:267, :463); project-state/STATUS.md Known issues (:534-536); DECISIONS 2026-09-15 row 94
* **State today:** The crawler half is done: runAll({ runId }) validates a caller uuid (bb_crawler.js:185-192, 606-611) and posts calendar last. The skill still registers after the crawl (SKILL.md:90-113), as row 94 decided. Prod transform_tick is 044's body: it folds a registered run on its calendar row or after 3 minutes with no new post (044:238-241), and the transform-request drain folds the newest registered run with no completeness check (044:296-301, 327). run_transform opens and closes its sync_runs row in one transaction, so all 35 rows have started_at = finished_at, 'running' is never visible, and the reaper has nothing to reap. Home shows only 'last synced'; the TopNav Sync button shows 'syncing…' while a request is claimed, with no age limit. v4 crawls take 20-26 s with gaps of 10 s or less.
* **Still missing:** (1) A driver migration (091+) re-created from 044: registered runs fold only on the calendar row; the idle branch stays for unregistered quarantine; the drain applies the same test. (2) A terminal rule for a registered crawl that never completes: fail the request and raise an attention item, or fold it labelled partial. (3) A running sync_runs row opened at claim, which run_transform and the fold filter adopt; finished_at from clock_timestamp(). (4) Home shows 'sync running' and 'interrupted'. (5) db/tests for the fold conditions. (6) SKILL steps 3/3a register first, under a DECISIONS row; crawler header, CADENCE_RUNBOOK :80-84, STATUS :534-536 and brief 82 C-3 step 3 (:122) updated.
* **Must respect:** [2026-09-15] "bb-sync keeps **registering `run_id` after `bb.runAll` returns**, even though the crawler now accepts a `runId`; register-first waits until `transform_tick` requires the `calendar` row for registered runs" · [2026-09-10] "The transform folds **only crawls registered on an owner-claimed `agent_requests` row** (`agent_requests.run_id`, migration 039); unregistered `bb_raw` runs are quarantined once, never folded; `bb_raw` unique on `(run_id, kind, bb_course_id)`" · [2026-09-10] "Transform runs as SQL functions on **pg_cron inside Postgres** (`transform_tick` every 2 min), not as an edge function or a local hub" · [2026-09-14] "iCal responses are collected by `ical_collect()` on every 2-minute tick, not by the daily poll (044)" · [2026-09-13] "`v_data_freshness` ignores `status = 'skipped'` rows and unregistered runs (migration 040; PM extended Phase 9's range by one number for it)" · [2026-09-15] "One open `sync` request at a time: while a `queued`/`claimed` sync exists, the Sync button re-copies its command instead of inserting another row" · [2026-09-11] "Only the newest crawl may declare a file missing (`stage_files` replay guard, 037)" · [2026-09-15] "Score-change counts (`scores_new`, `scores_changed`) are computed **only when the folded run is the newest registered crawl** (056, reusing 043's predicate); older runs report 0 with `older_run: true`" · [2026-09-16] "Phase 14 research calls, proposed (frozen when its PM session runs): the sync becomes a **deterministic Node + Playwright `sync-runner`**, no LLM in the common path"
* **Seams:** transform_tick (035/039/044, SECURITY DEFINER; latest 044, must keep ical_collect unless C-13 retires the poll); run_transform idempotence (051:136-145) and fold filter (044:236-237); agent_requests.run_id; bb_raw kinds (memberships, course ×7, calendar last); v_sync_status (035:537-557), freshnessLine (queries.sync.ts:546-556), NeedsAttention.tsx:96, useOpenSyncRequest (queries.sync.ts:1245); skills/bb-sync steps 2-4 and the desktop sync-terminal (same skill); Phase 14 sync-runner and its sync_claim / sync_register_run RPCs (82:121-137). Build before or with Phase 14 so the runner is written register-first.
* **Notes:** The partial-fold risk is theoretical today (largest gap between posts 10 s on v4 crawls, 16.7 s across all 8 retained runs, against a 3-minute trigger); the visible gaps are the missing 'running'/'interrupted' states and a dead crawl leaving the request 'claimed' and the button 'syncing…' forever. The 3-minute trigger is max(captured_at) older than 3 minutes, not 'one row older than three minutes' as SKILL.md:92-93, bb_crawler.js:86-87 and row 94's Why say. STATUS.md:534-536 is stale (runAll already takes runId; the tick is the blocker) and CADENCE_RUNBOOK.md:80-84 wrongly says run_transform opens the row before the first read. Brief 82 labels its sync_runner migration 090, which is taken. Per-run materials history, the guide's fourth sync-loop part, is C-12 because its outcome and size differ.

* **Seams:** Gates the sync side of the phase (E-1, E-3, E-5), per the brief's session prompt. An absolute probe is needed (location.origin + '/learn/api/public/v1/users/me') because Ultra's <base href> points at the CDN. pull_files.mjs:175 hard-codes local_path 'course context/…', so mount course-files there or pass the mirror root. The image uses the Python set pinned under E-1; the keep-alive probe loop is code inside E-1's runner. Cluster F items: containers-secrets-from-env; containers-umbrella-repo (bb2dash is PUBLIC and its .gitignore has no secrets/ line); containers-doctor (auth state belongs to doctor, not container health); containers-local-build-arm64-manifests; web-gitattributes-lf. The profile volume stays out of vault/RAG sync and unencrypted backups.
* **Notes:** The keep-alive item sits here rather than in E-1. Its deliverable is the measurement (task 0 plus task 2's overnight log), and the probe loop is one line of runner code. Its main payoff is the morning sync (E-7, still declined), so if that stays declined the probe can move to post-MVP. The asked_for called the spike 'frozen', but DECISIONS 2026-09-16 marks it proposed; the spike is what freezes it. Owner conflict: the task table gives task 2 to W-27, while the session prompts in brief 82 and ORCHESTRATOR have the PM run it before anything else. Stale session-prompt preconditions: 'after Phase 13 merges' in brief 82 (13 was skipped 2026-09-22) and 'create the ... vault repos' in both prompts (superseded by the 2026-09-24 realm row). The gate blocks only the sync side; harness tasks 8-9 do not need the login. The brief's names win over R1's (6901, bb-browser-profile) and R4's (browser-profile). R4 §4 recommends non-root with --no-sandbox and no SYS_ADMIN; the brief has not adopted it and its /security-review list does not name the sandbox flag. The VNC stack (x11vnc+noVNC or KasmVNC) is still open (R1 277-281). The Duo number takes 14 calendar days.

#### R-83 · Sync outcomes are reported from run data; failures raise one Inbox item
ingest · inbox · db · size M · **not built**
* **Source:** docs/planning/sprint-2/82_PHASE14_containers.md §C-3 step (6) (125), bullets 'Login expired' (126-128) and 'Dead-letter sweep' (129-130); §What the research changed 1 (44-49); §MVP (74-76); §DoD step 4 'Activity shows the report' (219-220) and bullet 3 'dead-letter' (228-229); §Task loops row 6 (260); §Open questions 1 and 3 (285-289); research R2 §3 (299-304) and §4 (388-406)
* **State today:** The skill path already handles an expired login: SKILL.md:39-59 checks location.href before claiming, fails the row with {"error":"SESSION EXPIRED"} and inserts one stack_must_confirm item. Requests 9 and 18 failed that way (claimed_at null) and produced one item, 282, through the open-row dedupe index (041). It stayed open 8 days through three clean syncs until inbox-apply request 37 archived it. The report's change lines already come from a template: sync_change_lines() (051:46) fills sync_runs.summary.changes, which Activity (queries.sync.ts:1106-1158) and the desktop toast read. Sync rows store only {run_id, status}; the rest of step 6 is chat prose. Nothing sweeps stale claims, and none has happened (claims lasted 2.5-4.3 min). raise_attention is executable by postgres and service_role only. Open attention rows: 0.
* **Still missing:** In the runner: a login_required branch (reason in result.error, login page left on the noVNC display, no retry); a sweep that flags claimed rows (any kind) older than 20 minutes once, never retrying; a pure report template over summary.changes, errors, failed stages and the 4b pulled/not-pulled list, stored by sync_close, its extra lines appended to sync_runs.summary.changes so Activity shows them. The login item raised inside sync_close; the dead-letter item through a separate DEFINER RPC, since the sweep closes nothing. Fixed, distinct refs for both. A release path so a dead-lettered claim stops blocking Sync. The login item closes itself once a later check passes. Tests: appears once, then clears.
* **Must respect:** [2026-09-14] "`attention_items` dedupes only while `state = 'open'` (041); a "Keep mine" answer stands until Blackboard's value changes (042); `applied_at` is set only when a fact was written, otherwise the row stays "answered, applies on next sync" (042)" · [2026-09-22] "**An answered Inbox row is processed once and archived: `attention_items.state = 'archived'` (090) with `archived_at`, `archived_by` and a `decision` record; `archive_attention_item()` is the only way in; `v_inbox_queue` is the worker's queue.** A state, not a second table" · [2026-09-22] "It flags feature changes and merges, raises new questions through `raise_attention()`, and never resolves an open row." · [2026-09-10] "Inbox resolutions carry a free-text `resolution_note` ("why") and are applied by the next transform, never directly; shapes: `conflict` `{accept}`, `stack_must_confirm`/`missing` `{value, value_type}`, `deadline`/`data_gap` `{dismissed}`" · [2026-09-15] "One open `sync` request at a time: while a `queued`/`claimed` sync exists, the Sync button re-copies its command instead of inserting another row" · [2026-09-15] "`my_submissions` rows are **exempt from `stage_files`' missing marker (053) and from `stage_gaps`' "bytes never stored" gap when they carry an `attempt_id` (054)**; step 4b reports files it could not pull in its own summary" · [2026-09-15] "Score-change counts (`scores_new`, `scores_changed`) are computed **only when the folded run is the newest registered crawl** (056, reusing 043's predicate); older runs report 0 with `older_run: true`" · [2026-09-10] "Sync cadence: **Stack triggers**, the app does the rest; no scheduled crawl, no reminders" · [2026-09-16] "Phase 14 research calls, proposed (frozen when its PM session runs): the sync becomes a **deterministic Node + Playwright `sync-runner`**, no LLM in the common path"
* **Seams:** Needs E-1 (the runner) and E-4 (sync_close's signature and the raise path). Touches attention_items (the kind check allows only conflict/missing/stack_must_confirm/deadline/data_gap, so reuse stack_must_confirm; missing/data_gap would be blocked for good by attention_answered()), attention_items_open_dedupe_idx (041:55-57), raise_attention (041:127-159), v_inbox_queue and inbox-apply's triage table, the Sync button's open-request lookup (queries.sync.ts:1050-1066), transform_tick's 30-minute quarantine hold (039:69-72), sync_runs.summary. The self-close clause shares one mechanism and one DECISIONS row with inbox-data-gap-auto-close (cluster B2). The desktop login toast is post-MVP (containers-post-mvp-queue, cluster F). skills/bb-sync step 1 should use the same ref while both paths are live.
* **Notes:** The report part is partly built (the change lines are live); the dead-letter sweep is not built at all. agent_requests has no reason column, so login_required goes in result; no web or desktop code reads 'SESSION EXPIRED', so renaming it breaks nothing. The Inbox cannot link http://localhost:6080 (sourceHref returns null for course-less rows, and 82 §Out of scope says 'any renderer change'), so the URL stays plain text. Nothing in web/ or desktop/ renders agent_requests.result, which is why the extra lines go into summary.changes; the desktop toast fires at fold time and will not see lines appended later. SKILL.md:58 still cites 'migration 031's unique key'; 041 replaced it. Self-closing the login item is a machine resolution, which SKILL.md:238 ('Never resolve an attention_items row on Stack's behalf') forbids today, so the shared DECISIONS row must state the exception. The MVP ties 'the Inbox tells me to log in again' to the scheduled sync (E-7, still declined); the button-press login branch does not depend on it. The 20-minute threshold is about 5x the longest past claim; re-check it once 4b runs inside the claim. A never-claimed queued row is not covered by the sweep (see E-7). PM default for the release path: answering or dismissing the dead-letter item cancels the request, done by the runner, since transform_tick never touches sync requests.

#### R-84 · The container syncs with a database credential that can do nothing else
db · containers · size M · **not built**
* **Source:** docs/planning/sprint-2/82_PHASE14_containers.md §C-3 bullet 'Database access, least privilege (migration 090)' (133-137); §C-6 secret sync_runner_db_url (185); §DoD bullet 'Migration 090 (+091)' (233-234) and no-secret/non-root bullet (240-241); §Task loops row 3 (257); §Workers W-27 (276); research R2 least-privilege options (336-352)
* **State today:** Prod has no sync_runner or agent_watcher role and no sync_claim, sync_register_run or sync_close (verifiers, 2026-09-24). The latest migration is 20260922165710 (090_attention_archive), and no migration contains `create role`. Today the skill claims, registers and closes through owner-level execute_sql; step 4b's bb_files updates are SQL that the owner runs. agent_requests has one owner-only RLS policy. v_sync_status and v_data_freshness are security_invoker views over owner-only tables. bb_files has only owner_all and anon_insert. Prod has 20 SECURITY DEFINER functions, each with a pinned search_path (19 use `public, pg_temp`); authenticated can call only app_owner and calendar_push_now, and raise_attention is postgres/service_role only. The grant-narrowing precedent is 038:46.
* **Still missing:** Migration 091 (not 090) and db/tests/phase14_091_sync_runner.sql. They create role sync_runner and DEFINER RPCs sync_claim(id), sync_register_run(id, run_id) and sync_close(id, state, report), each pinning `search_path = public, pg_temp`, revoked from public/anon/authenticated and granted only to sync_runner. The brief's grant list omits a queue read (queued rows; claims older than 20 minutes) and a raise path for Inbox items, and its reads and bb_files update need RLS policies or DEFINER wrappers, because the views are security_invoker and every table admits only authenticated. The work-list read needs EXECUTE on bb_file_relpath(). sync_close must allow queued→failed. sync_register_run must refuse non-sync, unclaimed or quarantined runs. Also: negative grant tests, a begin…rollback dry run, byte-identical apply, /security-review.
* **Must respect:** [2026-09-22] "**Sprint 2 planning follows the Phase 12b method** (list → ids → triage → researchers → one question batch → briefs); migration numbering continues from 091" · [2026-09-10] "The transform folds **only crawls registered on an owner-claimed `agent_requests` row** (`agent_requests.run_id`, migration 039); unregistered `bb_raw` runs are quarantined once, never folded; `bb_raw` unique on `(run_id, kind, bb_course_id)`" · [2026-09-10] "RLS hardened to owner-scoped (migration 020): 21 `using(true)` authenticated policies → `auth.uid() = public.app_owner()`" · [2026-09-11] "SECURITY DEFINER transform functions are not callable by `authenticated` (038); the app's only path to a transform is an `agent_requests` row" · [2026-09-10] "**All 15 public views are `security_invoker`** with anon revoked (migration 036, guard block refuses a future non-invoker view)" · [2026-09-03] "Crawler holds only the publishable key; insert-only RLS" · [2026-09-15] "`bb_files` anon INSERT is tightened to refuse `bucket = 'my_submissions'` and any `classified_by` of `stack` or `blackboard`; the Storage `bb_files_anon_insert` policy (bytes only) is **left as is**" · [2026-09-22] "The browser half stays a Playwright snippet in its header; the script never writes `bb_files` itself and hands the owner one `update` per file" · [2026-09-15] "One open `sync` request at a time: while a `queued`/`claimed` sync exists, the Sync button re-copies its command instead of inserting another row" · [2026-09-16] "`.env`-style secrets folder + Docker secrets" · [2026-09-16 (brief 82, Stack's decision #8; not a DECISIONS row)] "Secrets: one gitignored `.env` / secrets folder + Docker secrets at runtime. Never in an image" · [2026-09-16 (brief 82, Stack's decision #16; not a DECISIONS row)] "Guardrails: **$0**; today's non-Docker Windows path keeps working until acceptance; service key never in an image; "build it so that it is migratable later"" · [2026-09-16] "Phase 14 research calls, proposed (frozen when its PM session runs): the sync becomes a **deterministic Node + Playwright `sync-runner`**, no LLM in the common path"
* **Seams:** This is W-27's frozen seam: freeze the RPC signatures, with the queue read added, before branches are cut. It is a prerequisite of E-1 and E-3. E-7's insert function comes after it (092). Touches agent_requests, sync_runs/v_sync_status, attention_items, bb_files (storage_path, local_path, sha256, bytes, mime_type, downloaded_at, text_status, notes) and bb_file_relpath() (not DEFINER; unpinned search_path). Cluster C: sync_register_run is where ingest-register-first / db-tick-completeness-check (register first) and db-running-row-before-fetch (a running row at claim, which would give login items a sync_run_id) land, so freeze their semantics together. Cluster D: db-search-path-pin, db-secdef-authenticated-callable, db-sql-test-runner. The container must be able to reach the DSN (pooler); the secret is sync_runner_db_url (C-6).
* **Notes:** A role that can call sync_register_run is a second principal that can authorise folds. The 2026-09-10 'owner-claimed' row needs an amending DECISIONS row, and the change needs /security-review. agent_requests' table ACL still grants arwdxtm to anon and authenticated, so RLS is the only boundary a new policy must not widen. The brief still says 090/091 at lines 6, 133, 138, 233, 257 and 276; renumber to 091 (role) and 092 (scheduled job). Only the role is unblocked, since E-7 is still declined. A DSN for the owner's own container does not break the service-key ban (v3 D-4): the service key stays out. Pinning search_path is house rule and belongs in the DoD, not a separate task. Size M only if the extra policies/RPCs are counted; without them the role cannot poll or read status.

* **Source:** sprint-2/82_PHASE14_containers.md §Carried out of this phase bullet 1 (lines 302-307); sprint-2/research/82_RESEARCH_phase14_R6_harness_and_vault.md:453-477 (Secrets hygiene, 'A real gap'); agentic-harness hooks/README.md §Cloud sessions: /checkpoint

On harness main ae46b10 (PR #16 merged 2026-09-24, docs only), `git grep -i redact main -- skills/` returns nothing; skills/checkpoint/build-note.mjs imports only node built-ins (lines 19-24) and carries its own serializer copy but no redaction. The only guard is a prose rule for the model (SKILL.md:39, 'Never paste secrets, tokens, connection strings, or raw tool output'). Step 3 (SKILL.md:105-111) runs git add, commit and push of .harness/sessions, and the no-node fallback (SKILL.md:51-103) has the model write the whole note by hand. Redaction happens only downstream, in the collector (hooks/lib/checkpoints.mjs:38 import, :171 redact(body)), after the note is already pushed. bb2dash carries an installed copy at .claude/skills/checkpoint/ (hooks/install-checkpoint.mjs).
* **Still missing:** A redact pass in build-note.mjs over the body (and git-derived strings), or a refusal on a hit via findSecretValues, before the commit. redact.mjs has zero imports, so it can ship as a third payload file in install-checkpoint.mjs (PAYLOAD today ['SKILL.md','build-note.mjs'], SHA-256 checked); otherwise an inline copy with a rule-table pin test. A credential-seeded fixture test for build-note (JWT, sb_ key, connection string). A decision on the hand-written no-node fallback. The fixed skill re-installed into bb2dash's .claude/skills/checkpoint/.
* **Must respect:** [2026-09-16 (82_PHASE14_containers.md §Carried out of this phase, frozen brief text)] "**`/checkpoint` writes notes into a repo's git history with no secret redaction** — the SessionEnd hook and the collector both redact, `skills/checkpoint/build-note.mjs` does not (R6; verified by the PM: no `redact` reference under `skills/checkpoint/`). A harness fix, worth doing before Phase 14." · [2026-09-22 (harness docs/portable.md, frozen contract)] "**Redaction** happens at write time on every machine; a `local` realm never leaves it; the VM's machine file never carries the Supabase URL." · [2026-09-16] "**Phase 14 = R-28 containers, planned** (`82_PHASE14_containers.md`): portability first on this laptop" · [2026-09-09] "Workflow SOP: dev on branches, push per completed task, **one PR per phase**, merge only on Stack's word"
* **Seams:** Harness-owned code: skills/checkpoint/ (SKILL.md, build-note.mjs), hooks/lib/redact.mjs, hooks/install-checkpoint.mjs, tests checkpoint-build.test.mjs and install-checkpoint.test.mjs; bb2dash only re-installs its copy. The skill cannot import hooks/lib at runtime. Sequence: a standalone harness PR before W-28 (Phase 14) cuts its branch. G-10 decides whether the copied rules also load per-machine extras. The harness memory-sprint spec (merged as PR #16) plans the same guard for ~/.claude in R-H5: a scan with redact.mjs's rules before every commit that refuses on a hit, so one scanner can serve both.
* **Notes:** The leak is upstream of the vault: a note lands in the source repo's history (bb2dash or agentic-harness) before the collector redacts it, so no realm-side scanner would close it. Only the 82 brief text adopts the fix; no DECISIONS row does yet.

## 2. PM-added requirements

Intermediate steps sprint 1's leftovers cannot ship without: a seam, a data fix, a test harness,
a migration. Each is marked *PM-added*, says in one line why, and names the research note that
backs it where the PM had to look something up. Stage B appends *research-added* rows here.

65 steps as drafted, numbered P-n; "for R-nn" names the carried requirement each one unblocks. PM curation (2026-09-24), ids kept stable: P-21 folds into P-18 (one migration-number allocation); P-36 into P-22 and P-37 into P-23 (one signed-CDN fetch, one embed step); the seven state-doc refresh steps (P-5, P-13, P-20, P-29, P-32, P-41, P-62) are one Stage D task on this branch, not seven phase tasks; P-3, P-4, P-34, P-64, P-65 are DECISIONS rows Stage B's question batch resolves. Stage B appends *research-added* rows below.

#### P-1 · GEO.103 attendance columns stop producing a made-up 0.0% grade *(PM-added)*

## 3. Stack's new features and requirements

Pasted by Stack on 2026-09-23 (before Stage A closed) and transcribed verbatim by the PM; his
three headings are kept. Ids follow the intake convention `S2-<area>-<n>`. Fields Stack did not write
(**why**, **must / should**, **acceptance**, **must not**) are left as "_to confirm_" and are
put to him with a proposed default in Stage B's single question batch — nothing is invented
here. Lines marked *PM note* are the PM's reading, not Stack's words.

Template per entry:

```
### S2-<area>-<n> · <short title>
* **what (Stack's words):** …
* **why:** …
* **must / should:** must | should
* **acceptance (Stack's words):** what he sees, clicks or reads that proves it is done
* **must not:** anything it must not do or change
```

### S2-workspace-1 · workspace section: a chatbot interface that routes prompts by task complexity
* **what (Stack's words):** workspace section: functions as a chatbot interface that works by
  routing prompts to different agent model levels depending on the complexity of the task. Low
  effort for simple rag db queries or pulling of documents, higher models for the actual
  execution of the tasks. Currently I want to build it as if I might route it to either a local
  model or to a frontier model. Claude is the only one we will actually hook up but I wish to
  build the functionality. Ideally use my subscription to run it instead of having to pay for
  api credits.
* **why:** _to confirm_
* **must / should:** _to confirm_
* **acceptance (Stack's words):** _to confirm_
* **must not:** _to confirm_
* *PM note:* this reverses §4 D-1 ("in-app chat assistant", declined in Requirements v2 §5) and
  needs a DECISIONS row when adopted. "Use my subscription" points at the Claude Code CLI path
  (`claude -p` with a `claude setup-token` token, which Phase 14's research R2 §1 already cleared
  for the unmodified binary) rather than the API; Stage B researches the router, the
  local-or-frontier seam, and where the process runs (it is a container job, so it sits on
  Phase 14's seams).

## 6. Open questions carried to Stage B's batch

Product calls the record cannot settle. Each carries the default the PM will take if Stack says nothing; Stage B's synthesis puts them to him in one batch with the research-added questions.

1. **Presentation dates: was 11/4 your SITN date or your individual slot; which individual slot (9/30, 10/14, 10/26, 11/4, 11/16); Major Case 1 day (10/20, 10/21 or 10/22); Major Case 2 day (11/17 or 11/19)?** (A) — default: Keep SITN on 11/4 as recorded, leave the individual presentation undated and tentative, and set major-project-1 back to tentative to match major-project-2.. Why: Nothing may be invented (line 110); major-project-1's 'confirmed' came from a 'keep' against a stale 2022 date, not a known day. The first individual slot is 2026-09-30.
2. **GEO.103 lecture 'Absences' and recitation 'Attendance' both read 0/100 and count as graded attendance, so GEO's figure works out to 0.0%. Are these absence counts?** (A) — default: Mark both 'Not graded' with the picker until the GEO sitting settles how attendance is scored; GEO then shows 'nothing graded yet'.. Why: CLAUDE.md 'No fabricated numbers anywhere'; the worker's own item 157 calls the lecture column a cumulative absence count; a picker link needs no migration.
3. **Un-stub V-1 in sprint 2 (six sittings with you, IST.323 first), or keep it stubbed and carry only the data calls in this batch?** (A) — default: Un-stub it inside the sprint-2 grades phase; sitting 1 (IST.323) runs once the launcher works and the export is regenerated.. Why: DECISIONS 2026-09-16 line 124 stubbed it and ORCHESTRATOR §4 line 195 leaves the restart to you; A-3 to A-7 wait on it, and IST.323's 13-pt column is graded 2026-12-03.
4. **IST.323 'Proposal and Appendices' (13 pts = proposal 11 + completed log 2): was your 2026-09-22 link to Proposal the answer, and should the parts become Proposal 13 / Running Log 1 (final project still 20)?** (A) — default: Re-cut components 18/19 to 13/1 by UPDATE in the reconciliation migration and point fp-log-final at component 18 or mark it bookkeeping; decided in the IST.323 sitting before 2026-12-03.. Why: The engine cannot split one column across two parts; the syllabus says the completed log is submitted with the proposal and component 18's notes already record 11 + 2, so the re-cut keeps final_project at 20 and lets graded so far match Blackboard once scored; keeping 11/3/6 leaves a permanent 2-point gap.

The syllabus has Attendance 150 and Participation 100.** (A) — default: Leave today's links; the two unlinked columns are named under the figure once scored and get linked with the picker then.. Why: None of the three is scored (sync 62), so nothing is wrong on screen yet, and the 2021 column metadata is stale-shell (line 172) and gives no signal.
6. **How V-1 marks a checked row: (a) where 'checked against the materials on <date>' lives; (b) whether rows you answer from memory, and links the Inbox worker confirmed from context, are STACK_OVERRIDE + confirmed or tentative until a document backs them; (c) whether the grading invariants run by hand or also in every sync?** (A) — default: (a) notes for schemes and components, source_ref for assignments, as 'bb_file:<id>#unit:<n>' plus the date, no schema change; (b) STACK_OVERRIDE + confirmed with your why, the worker's links re-checked in the sittings, and links on rows whose confidence tracks a date confirmed through grade_column_links; (c) by hand in the V-1 PR, later a bb-sync step that reports through raise_attention and never blocks the fold.. Why: These are research 75 §6 Q2, Q3 and Q5, routed to you by the 2026-09-14 research row and never answered. assignments has no notes column; tentative lets the next sync overwrite dates and points silently (084 gates); no CI exists. Brief 63's DoD already assumes these defaults.

Why: The rule line needs no engine change and can be walked after Exam 1 (2026-10-01); per-exam weights cannot show on real data before Exam 3 on 2026-12-08, inside the freeze.
